# Listing Kit: Vanilla Quality of Life

Tap the Copy button on a block, then paste it into the CurseForge listing. Generated from DESCRIPTION.md: edit that, not this file.

## Package Name

```text
Vanilla Quality of Life
```

## Summary (130/256)

```text
Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.
```

## Description (HTML)

Choose any of the 5 themes below. Tap the Copy button on your preferred theme and paste into the CurseForge description editor in source (HTML) mode.

### Theme 1: Vanilla Adaptive (Default)

Theme-neutral layout following the viewer's CurseForge light or dark mode.

```html
<div><div style="border:2px solid #15803d;border-radius:18px;line-height:1.5"><br><p style="text-align:center"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Vanilla-Quality-of-Life/assets/banner.png" alt="Vanilla Quality of Life" style="width:100%"></p><p style="color:#8a94a3;font-size:14px;text-align:center">by ALNStudios</p><p style="text-align:center"><span style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:16px">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p><p style="text-align:center;margin-left:16px;margin-right:16px">Adds automatic tool and weapon switching, Tree Capitator, Domino Ore Miner, offhand quick-swap with moving held light, extra leashable mobs, new recipes, and Wandering Trader collector trades.</p><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-left:14px;margin-right:14px"><div style="background-color:#dcfce7;color:#14532d;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Bedrock</strong>: 1.26+&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#dcfce7;color:#14532d;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Experimental</strong>: No&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#dcfce7;color:#14532d;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Achievements</strong>: Yes, achievement-friendly&nbsp;&nbsp;</div></div><br></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="About"></p>
<p>Hit a block and the best tool from your hotbar swaps into Slot 1, then returns a moment after you stop. Sneak while chopping a log or mining an ore to fell the whole tree or take the connected vein.</p>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/what_you_get.png" alt="What You Get"></p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>With Slot 1 selected, hitting a block or mob swaps the best tool from your hotbar into Slot 1, and the item that was there returns a moment after you stop.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tools close to breaking are avoided, and a Mending tool that needs repair wins when two tools are equally good.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Capitator</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Sneak while chopping a log with an Axe to fell the whole tree, up to 128 logs, with the drops merged into full stacks within 5-block areas.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, fences, glass, bricks, or storage are left standing, and logs more than 8 blocks sideways from the broken log are ignored.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Replanting</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>After a whole tree is felled from its base, a matching sapling is planted where the trunk stood, using one sapling from your inventory for each spot.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> It works for Oak, Spruce, Birch, Jungle, Acacia, Dark Oak, Cherry, and Pale Oak on dirt, grass, podzol, coarse dirt, rooted dirt, moss, mud, or mycelium, plants up to 4 saplings for wide trunks, and costs nothing in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Leaf Clearing</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>When Tree Capitator fells a whole tree, that tree's leaves are cleared too, and they still drop some saplings, sticks, and apples (Oak and Dark Oak only).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Leaves still holding on to another tree of the same wood stay in place, and nothing drops in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Faster Crop Growth</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>The first time the pack loads in a world, it sets the random tick speed to 4, so crops and saplings grow faster.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> This is applied once per world, and you can change it afterward with the /gamerule command.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Domino Ore Miner</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Sneak while mining an ore with a Pickaxe to harvest the connected vein, up to 64 ore blocks, or 4 for Ancient Debris.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Ores give their normal experience without Silk Touch, as up to 10 orbs per chain with any extra going straight to you.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Mining Presets</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Three presets in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings choose how chain mining starts: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, and Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Changing the preset may need the pack reactivated or the world reloaded.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool Durability Guard</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Chain mining stops when your tool has 0 durability left, with a warning sound and an action-bar alert.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> A tool at 0 durability breaks only the single block you hit and does not start Tree Capitator or Domino Ore Miner.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Offhand Quick-Swap and Held Light</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Double-sneak while standing still, or sneak and use in the air, to move your held item into your offhand.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Universal Leads</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Leads work on Villagers, Iron Golems, Creepers, Endermen, Ghasts, and most other mobs, while the Warden, Wither, and Ender Dragon cannot be leashed.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Pet and Mount Protection</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span>Tamed Wolves, Cats, Parrots, Horses, Donkeys, Mules, and Llamas take no damage from player melee attacks, sweeping strikes, and arrows.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8a94a3"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tamed animals are also immune to Creeper and TNT explosion damage.</span></p></div></div></div>

<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/how_to_play.png" alt="Get Started"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Import the complete add-on and enable both included packs in your world settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Choose a mining preset in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, or Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Put your tools in the hotbar, select Slot 1, and hit blocks or mobs to see the best tool swap in.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">4</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Sneak while breaking a log with an Axe or an ore with a Pickaxe to fell the tree or mine the vein.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="Common Questions"></p>
<div class="spoiler" title="Why did a tree stay standing?" data-title="Why did a tree stay standing?"><p><strong>Why did a tree stay standing?</strong><br>Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, or fences are skipped, and a very large tree is cut off at 128 logs.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?" data-title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?"><p><strong>Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?</strong><br>Auto-switch only uses tools in your hotbar.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?" data-title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?"><p><strong>Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?</strong><br>A sapling is planted only when the whole tree was felled, the trunk stood on a supported soil block, and you carry a matching sapling.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?" data-title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?"><p><strong>What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?</strong><br>Chain mining stops, and the next use of that tool breaks it.</p></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/install.png" alt="Install"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #8a94a3;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Download the file from this page and open it with Minecraft.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Create or edit a world and turn on the new pack(s) in its settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#15803d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;">Play. ### Theme 2: Netherite Stealth (Modern Dark)
```

High-tech dark obsidian & emerald storefront.

```html
<div style="background-color:#0d1117;color:#c9d1d9;border-radius:12px;padding-left:12px"><div style="margin-right:12px"><br><div style="background-color:#161b22;border:2px solid #10b981;border-radius:18px;line-height:1.5"><br><p style="text-align:center"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Vanilla-Quality-of-Life/assets/banner.png" alt="Vanilla Quality of Life" style="width:100%"></p><p style="color:#8b949e;font-size:14px;text-align:center">by ALNStudios</p><p style="text-align:center"><span style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:16px">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p><p style="color:#8b949e;text-align:center;margin-left:16px;margin-right:16px">Adds automatic tool and weapon switching, Tree Capitator, Domino Ore Miner, offhand quick-swap with moving held light, extra leashable mobs, new recipes, and Wandering Trader collector trades.</p><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-left:14px;margin-right:14px"><div style="background-color:#161b22;color:#10b981;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Bedrock</strong>: 1.26+&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#161b22;color:#10b981;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Experimental</strong>: No&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#161b22;color:#10b981;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Achievements</strong>: Yes, achievement-friendly&nbsp;&nbsp;</div></div><br></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="About"></p>
<p style="color:#c9d1d9">Hit a block and the best tool from your hotbar swaps into Slot 1, then returns a moment after you stop. Sneak while chopping a log or mining an ore to fell the whole tree or take the connected vein.</p>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/what_you_get.png" alt="What You Get"></p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">With Slot 1 selected, hitting a block or mob swaps the best tool from your hotbar into Slot 1, and the item that was there returns a moment after you stop.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tools close to breaking are avoided, and a Mending tool that needs repair wins when two tools are equally good.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Capitator</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Sneak while chopping a log with an Axe to fell the whole tree, up to 128 logs, with the drops merged into full stacks within 5-block areas.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, fences, glass, bricks, or storage are left standing, and logs more than 8 blocks sideways from the broken log are ignored.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Replanting</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">After a whole tree is felled from its base, a matching sapling is planted where the trunk stood, using one sapling from your inventory for each spot.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> It works for Oak, Spruce, Birch, Jungle, Acacia, Dark Oak, Cherry, and Pale Oak on dirt, grass, podzol, coarse dirt, rooted dirt, moss, mud, or mycelium, plants up to 4 saplings for wide trunks, and costs nothing in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Leaf Clearing</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">When Tree Capitator fells a whole tree, that tree's leaves are cleared too, and they still drop some saplings, sticks, and apples (Oak and Dark Oak only).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Leaves still holding on to another tree of the same wood stay in place, and nothing drops in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Faster Crop Growth</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">The first time the pack loads in a world, it sets the random tick speed to 4, so crops and saplings grow faster.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> This is applied once per world, and you can change it afterward with the /gamerule command.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Domino Ore Miner</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Sneak while mining an ore with a Pickaxe to harvest the connected vein, up to 64 ore blocks, or 4 for Ancient Debris.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Ores give their normal experience without Silk Touch, as up to 10 orbs per chain with any extra going straight to you.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Mining Presets</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Three presets in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings choose how chain mining starts: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, and Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Changing the preset may need the pack reactivated or the world reloaded.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool Durability Guard</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Chain mining stops when your tool has 0 durability left, with a warning sound and an action-bar alert.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> A tool at 0 durability breaks only the single block you hit and does not start Tree Capitator or Domino Ore Miner.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Offhand Quick-Swap and Held Light</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Double-sneak while standing still, or sneak and use in the air, to move your held item into your offhand.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Universal Leads</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Leads work on Villagers, Iron Golems, Creepers, Endermen, Ghasts, and most other mobs, while the Warden, Wither, and Ender Dragon cannot be leashed.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Pet and Mount Protection</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#c9d1d9">Tamed Wolves, Cats, Parrots, Horses, Donkeys, Mules, and Llamas take no damage from player melee attacks, sweeping strikes, and arrows.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#8b949e"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tamed animals are also immune to Creeper and TNT explosion damage.</span></p></div></div></div>

<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/how_to_play.png" alt="Get Started"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Import the complete add-on and enable both included packs in your world settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Choose a mining preset in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, or Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Put your tools in the hotbar, select Slot 1, and hit blocks or mobs to see the best tool swap in.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">4</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Sneak while breaking a log with an Axe or an ore with a Pickaxe to fell the tree or mine the vein.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="Common Questions"></p>
<div class="spoiler" title="Why did a tree stay standing?" data-title="Why did a tree stay standing?"><p style="color:#c9d1d9"><strong>Why did a tree stay standing?</strong><br>Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, or fences are skipped, and a very large tree is cut off at 128 logs.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?" data-title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?"><p style="color:#c9d1d9"><strong>Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?</strong><br>Auto-switch only uses tools in your hotbar.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?" data-title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?"><p style="color:#c9d1d9"><strong>Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?</strong><br>A sapling is planted only when the whole tree was felled, the trunk stood on a supported soil block, and you carry a matching sapling.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?" data-title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?"><p style="color:#c9d1d9"><strong>What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?</strong><br>Chain mining stops, and the next use of that tool breaks it.</p></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/install.png" alt="Install"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Download the file from this page and open it with Minecraft.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Create or edit a world and turn on the new pack(s) in its settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#c9d1d9">Play. No Experimental toggles needed.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div style="background-color:#161b22;border:1px solid #30363d;border-radius:16px;text-align:center"><br><div style="background-color:#10b981;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:15px;line-height:2;display:inline-block">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Found a Bug or Have a Suggestion?</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><p style="font-size:14px;color:#c9d1d9">Please leave a comment on this project page! All bug reports, gameplay feedback, and pack suggestions are welcome and appreciated.</p></div>
```

### Theme 3: Bedrock GUI (In-Game Menu)

Authentic Minecraft stone dialog with 3D beveled borders and item slot insets.

```html
<div style="background-color:#313233;color:#e0e0e0;border-radius:12px;padding-left:12px"><div style="margin-right:12px"><br><div style="background-color:#202021;border:2px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:18px;line-height:1.5"><br><p style="text-align:center"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Vanilla-Quality-of-Life/assets/banner.png" alt="Vanilla Quality of Life" style="width:100%"></p><p style="color:#a0a0a0;font-size:14px;text-align:center">by ALNStudios</p><p style="text-align:center"><span style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:16px">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p><p style="color:#d0d0d0;text-align:center;margin-left:16px;margin-right:16px">Adds automatic tool and weapon switching, Tree Capitator, Domino Ore Miner, offhand quick-swap with moving held light, extra leashable mobs, new recipes, and Wandering Trader collector trades.</p><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-left:14px;margin-right:14px"><div style="background-color:#202021;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Bedrock</strong>: 1.26+&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#202021;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Experimental</strong>: No&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#202021;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Achievements</strong>: Yes, achievement-friendly&nbsp;&nbsp;</div></div><br></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="About"></p>
<p style="color:#e0e0e0">Hit a block and the best tool from your hotbar swaps into Slot 1, then returns a moment after you stop. Sneak while chopping a log or mining an ore to fell the whole tree or take the connected vein.</p>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/what_you_get.png" alt="What You Get"></p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">With Slot 1 selected, hitting a block or mob swaps the best tool from your hotbar into Slot 1, and the item that was there returns a moment after you stop.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tools close to breaking are avoided, and a Mending tool that needs repair wins when two tools are equally good.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Capitator</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Sneak while chopping a log with an Axe to fell the whole tree, up to 128 logs, with the drops merged into full stacks within 5-block areas.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, fences, glass, bricks, or storage are left standing, and logs more than 8 blocks sideways from the broken log are ignored.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Replanting</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">After a whole tree is felled from its base, a matching sapling is planted where the trunk stood, using one sapling from your inventory for each spot.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> It works for Oak, Spruce, Birch, Jungle, Acacia, Dark Oak, Cherry, and Pale Oak on dirt, grass, podzol, coarse dirt, rooted dirt, moss, mud, or mycelium, plants up to 4 saplings for wide trunks, and costs nothing in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Leaf Clearing</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">When Tree Capitator fells a whole tree, that tree's leaves are cleared too, and they still drop some saplings, sticks, and apples (Oak and Dark Oak only).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Leaves still holding on to another tree of the same wood stay in place, and nothing drops in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Faster Crop Growth</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">The first time the pack loads in a world, it sets the random tick speed to 4, so crops and saplings grow faster.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> This is applied once per world, and you can change it afterward with the /gamerule command.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Domino Ore Miner</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Sneak while mining an ore with a Pickaxe to harvest the connected vein, up to 64 ore blocks, or 4 for Ancient Debris.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Ores give their normal experience without Silk Touch, as up to 10 orbs per chain with any extra going straight to you.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Mining Presets</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Three presets in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings choose how chain mining starts: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, and Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Changing the preset may need the pack reactivated or the world reloaded.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool Durability Guard</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Chain mining stops when your tool has 0 durability left, with a warning sound and an action-bar alert.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> A tool at 0 durability breaks only the single block you hit and does not start Tree Capitator or Domino Ore Miner.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Offhand Quick-Swap and Held Light</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Double-sneak while standing still, or sneak and use in the air, to move your held item into your offhand.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Universal Leads</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Leads work on Villagers, Iron Golems, Creepers, Endermen, Ghasts, and most other mobs, while the Warden, Wither, and Ender Dragon cannot be leashed.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Pet and Mount Protection</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#e0e0e0">Tamed Wolves, Cats, Parrots, Horses, Donkeys, Mules, and Llamas take no damage from player melee attacks, sweeping strikes, and arrows.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#a0a0a0"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tamed animals are also immune to Creeper and TNT explosion damage.</span></p></div></div></div>

<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/how_to_play.png" alt="Get Started"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Import the complete add-on and enable both included packs in your world settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Choose a mining preset in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, or Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Put your tools in the hotbar, select Slot 1, and hit blocks or mobs to see the best tool swap in.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">4</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Sneak while breaking a log with an Axe or an ore with a Pickaxe to fell the tree or mine the vein.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="Common Questions"></p>
<div class="spoiler" title="Why did a tree stay standing?" data-title="Why did a tree stay standing?"><p style="color:#e0e0e0"><strong>Why did a tree stay standing?</strong><br>Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, or fences are skipped, and a very large tree is cut off at 128 logs.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?" data-title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?"><p style="color:#e0e0e0"><strong>Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?</strong><br>Auto-switch only uses tools in your hotbar.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?" data-title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?"><p style="color:#e0e0e0"><strong>Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?</strong><br>A sapling is planted only when the whole tree was felled, the trunk stood on a supported soil block, and you carry a matching sapling.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?" data-title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?"><p style="color:#e0e0e0"><strong>What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?</strong><br>Chain mining stops, and the next use of that tool breaks it.</p></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/install.png" alt="Install"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Download the file from this page and open it with Minecraft.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Create or edit a world and turn on the new pack(s) in its settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#e0e0e0">Play. No Experimental toggles needed.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div style="background-color:#202021;border:1px solid #4a4b4d;border-radius:16px;text-align:center"><br><div style="background-color:#4a4b4d;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:15px;line-height:2;display:inline-block">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Found a Bug or Have a Suggestion?</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><p style="font-size:14px;color:#e0e0e0">Please leave a comment on this project page! All bug reports, gameplay feedback, and pack suggestions are welcome and appreciated.</p></div>
```

### Theme 4: Emerald Marketplace

Official Minecraft Marketplace DLC showcase look with gold and emerald badges.

```html
<div style="background-color:#022c22;color:#ecfdf5;border-radius:12px;padding-left:12px"><div style="margin-right:12px"><br><div style="background-color:#064e3b;border:2px solid #059669;border-radius:18px;line-height:1.5"><br><p style="text-align:center"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Vanilla-Quality-of-Life/assets/banner.png" alt="Vanilla Quality of Life" style="width:100%"></p><p style="color:#6ee7b7;font-size:14px;text-align:center">by ALNStudios</p><p style="text-align:center"><span style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:16px">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p><p style="color:#a7f3d0;text-align:center;margin-left:16px;margin-right:16px">Adds automatic tool and weapon switching, Tree Capitator, Domino Ore Miner, offhand quick-swap with moving held light, extra leashable mobs, new recipes, and Wandering Trader collector trades.</p><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-left:14px;margin-right:14px"><div style="background-color:#047857;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Bedrock</strong>: 1.26+&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#047857;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Experimental</strong>: No&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#047857;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Achievements</strong>: Yes, achievement-friendly&nbsp;&nbsp;</div></div><br></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="About"></p>
<p style="color:#ecfdf5">Hit a block and the best tool from your hotbar swaps into Slot 1, then returns a moment after you stop. Sneak while chopping a log or mining an ore to fell the whole tree or take the connected vein.</p>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/what_you_get.png" alt="What You Get"></p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">With Slot 1 selected, hitting a block or mob swaps the best tool from your hotbar into Slot 1, and the item that was there returns a moment after you stop.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tools close to breaking are avoided, and a Mending tool that needs repair wins when two tools are equally good.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Capitator</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Sneak while chopping a log with an Axe to fell the whole tree, up to 128 logs, with the drops merged into full stacks within 5-block areas.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, fences, glass, bricks, or storage are left standing, and logs more than 8 blocks sideways from the broken log are ignored.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Replanting</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">After a whole tree is felled from its base, a matching sapling is planted where the trunk stood, using one sapling from your inventory for each spot.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> It works for Oak, Spruce, Birch, Jungle, Acacia, Dark Oak, Cherry, and Pale Oak on dirt, grass, podzol, coarse dirt, rooted dirt, moss, mud, or mycelium, plants up to 4 saplings for wide trunks, and costs nothing in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Leaf Clearing</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">When Tree Capitator fells a whole tree, that tree's leaves are cleared too, and they still drop some saplings, sticks, and apples (Oak and Dark Oak only).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Leaves still holding on to another tree of the same wood stay in place, and nothing drops in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Faster Crop Growth</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">The first time the pack loads in a world, it sets the random tick speed to 4, so crops and saplings grow faster.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> This is applied once per world, and you can change it afterward with the /gamerule command.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Domino Ore Miner</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Sneak while mining an ore with a Pickaxe to harvest the connected vein, up to 64 ore blocks, or 4 for Ancient Debris.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Ores give their normal experience without Silk Touch, as up to 10 orbs per chain with any extra going straight to you.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Mining Presets</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Three presets in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings choose how chain mining starts: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, and Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Changing the preset may need the pack reactivated or the world reloaded.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool Durability Guard</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Chain mining stops when your tool has 0 durability left, with a warning sound and an action-bar alert.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> A tool at 0 durability breaks only the single block you hit and does not start Tree Capitator or Domino Ore Miner.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Offhand Quick-Swap and Held Light</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Double-sneak while standing still, or sneak and use in the air, to move your held item into your offhand.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Universal Leads</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Leads work on Villagers, Iron Golems, Creepers, Endermen, Ghasts, and most other mobs, while the Warden, Wither, and Ender Dragon cannot be leashed.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Pet and Mount Protection</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#ecfdf5">Tamed Wolves, Cats, Parrots, Horses, Donkeys, Mules, and Llamas take no damage from player melee attacks, sweeping strikes, and arrows.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#6ee7b7"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tamed animals are also immune to Creeper and TNT explosion damage.</span></p></div></div></div>

<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/how_to_play.png" alt="Get Started"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Import the complete add-on and enable both included packs in your world settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Choose a mining preset in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, or Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Put your tools in the hotbar, select Slot 1, and hit blocks or mobs to see the best tool swap in.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">4</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Sneak while breaking a log with an Axe or an ore with a Pickaxe to fell the tree or mine the vein.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="Common Questions"></p>
<div class="spoiler" title="Why did a tree stay standing?" data-title="Why did a tree stay standing?"><p style="color:#ecfdf5"><strong>Why did a tree stay standing?</strong><br>Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, or fences are skipped, and a very large tree is cut off at 128 logs.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?" data-title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?"><p style="color:#ecfdf5"><strong>Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?</strong><br>Auto-switch only uses tools in your hotbar.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?" data-title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?"><p style="color:#ecfdf5"><strong>Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?</strong><br>A sapling is planted only when the whole tree was felled, the trunk stood on a supported soil block, and you carry a matching sapling.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?" data-title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?"><p style="color:#ecfdf5"><strong>What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?</strong><br>Chain mining stops, and the next use of that tool breaks it.</p></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/install.png" alt="Install"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Download the file from this page and open it with Minecraft.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Create or edit a world and turn on the new pack(s) in its settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#ecfdf5">Play. No Experimental toggles needed.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div style="background-color:#064e3b;border:1px solid #059669;border-radius:16px;text-align:center"><br><div style="background-color:#059669;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:15px;line-height:2;display:inline-block">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Found a Bug or Have a Suggestion?</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><p style="font-size:14px;color:#ecfdf5">Please leave a comment on this project page! ### Theme 5: Arcane Grimoire (Fantasy RPG)
```

Midnight obsidian purple theme with mystic amethyst and enchantment accents.

```html
<div style="background-color:#120c1f;color:#f3e8ff;border-radius:12px;padding-left:12px"><div style="margin-right:12px"><br><div style="background-color:#1c1033;border:2px solid #7e22ce;border-radius:18px;line-height:1.5"><br><p style="text-align:center"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Vanilla-Quality-of-Life/assets/banner.png" alt="Vanilla Quality of Life" style="width:100%"></p><p style="color:#c084fc;font-size:14px;text-align:center">by ALNStudios</p><p style="text-align:center"><span style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:16px">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></p><p style="color:#d8b4fe;text-align:center;margin-left:16px;margin-right:16px">Adds automatic tool and weapon switching, Tree Capitator, Domino Ore Miner, offhand quick-swap with moving held light, extra leashable mobs, new recipes, and Wandering Trader collector trades.</p><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-left:14px;margin-right:14px"><div style="background-color:#3b0764;color:#f3e8ff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Bedrock</strong>: 1.26+&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#3b0764;color:#f3e8ff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Experimental</strong>: No&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><div style="background-color:#3b0764;color:#f3e8ff;border-radius:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Achievements</strong>: Yes, achievement-friendly&nbsp;&nbsp;</div></div><br></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="About"></p>
<p style="color:#f3e8ff">Hit a block and the best tool from your hotbar swaps into Slot 1, then returns a moment after you stop. Sneak while chopping a log or mining an ore to fell the whole tree or take the connected vein.</p>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/what_you_get.png" alt="What You Get"></p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">With Slot 1 selected, hitting a block or mob swaps the best tool from your hotbar into Slot 1, and the item that was there returns a moment after you stop.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tools close to breaking are avoided, and a Mending tool that needs repair wins when two tools are equally good.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Capitator</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Sneak while chopping a log with an Axe to fell the whole tree, up to 128 logs, with the drops merged into full stacks within 5-block areas.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, fences, glass, bricks, or storage are left standing, and logs more than 8 blocks sideways from the broken log are ignored.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tree Replanting</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">After a whole tree is felled from its base, a matching sapling is planted where the trunk stood, using one sapling from your inventory for each spot.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> It works for Oak, Spruce, Birch, Jungle, Acacia, Dark Oak, Cherry, and Pale Oak on dirt, grass, podzol, coarse dirt, rooted dirt, moss, mud, or mycelium, plants up to 4 saplings for wide trunks, and costs nothing in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Leaf Clearing</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">When Tree Capitator fells a whole tree, that tree's leaves are cleared too, and they still drop some saplings, sticks, and apples (Oak and Dark Oak only).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Leaves still holding on to another tree of the same wood stay in place, and nothing drops in Creative.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Faster Crop Growth</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">The first time the pack loads in a world, it sets the random tick speed to 4, so crops and saplings grow faster.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> This is applied once per world, and you can change it afterward with the /gamerule command.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Domino Ore Miner</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Sneak while mining an ore with a Pickaxe to harvest the connected vein, up to 64 ore blocks, or 4 for Ancient Debris.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Ores give their normal experience without Silk Touch, as up to 10 orbs per chain with any extra going straight to you.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Mining Presets</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Three presets in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings choose how chain mining starts: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, and Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Changing the preset may need the pack reactivated or the world reloaded.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Tool Durability Guard</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Chain mining stops when your tool has 0 durability left, with a warning sound and an action-bar alert.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> A tool at 0 durability breaks only the single block you hit and does not start Tree Capitator or Domino Ore Miner.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Offhand Quick-Swap and Held Light</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Double-sneak while standing still, or sneak and use in the air, to move your held item into your offhand.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Universal Leads</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Leads work on Villagers, Iron Golems, Creepers, Endermen, Ghasts, and most other mobs, while the Warden, Wither, and Ender Dragon cannot be leashed.</span></p></div></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:16px;font-size:16px;line-height:2.2"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;Pet and Mount Protection</strong></div><div style="margin-left:12px;margin-right:12px"><p><span style="color:#f3e8ff">Tamed Wolves, Cats, Parrots, Horses, Donkeys, Mules, and Llamas take no damage from player melee attacks, sweeping strikes, and arrows.</span><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#c084fc"><strong>Good To Know:</strong> Tamed animals are also immune to Creeper and TNT explosion damage.</span></p></div></div></div>

<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/how_to_play.png" alt="Get Started"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Import the complete add-on and enable both included packs in your world settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Choose a mining preset in the Behavior Pack's subpack settings: Sneak to Chain Mine (Default), Auto Chain Mine: Tool in Slot 1, or Chain Mining Off (Other Features On).</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Put your tools in the hotbar, select Slot 1, and hit blocks or mobs to see the best tool swap in.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">4</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Sneak while breaking a log with an Axe or an ore with a Pickaxe to fell the tree or mine the vein.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/about.png" alt="Common Questions"></p>
<div class="spoiler" title="Why did a tree stay standing?" data-title="Why did a tree stay standing?"><p style="color:#f3e8ff"><strong>Why did a tree stay standing?</strong><br>Trees touching player-built blocks such as planks, doors, or fences are skipped, and a very large tree is cut off at 128 logs.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?" data-title="Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?"><p style="color:#f3e8ff"><strong>Why did auto-switch ignore a tool in my backpack?</strong><br>Auto-switch only uses tools in your hotbar.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?" data-title="Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?"><p style="color:#f3e8ff"><strong>Why was no sapling planted after I felled a tree?</strong><br>A sapling is planted only when the whole tree was felled, the trunk stood on a supported soil block, and you carry a matching sapling.</p></div><div class="spoiler" title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?" data-title="What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?"><p style="color:#f3e8ff"><strong>What happens when a tool reaches 0 durability?</strong><br>Chain mining stops, and the next use of that tool breaks it.</p></div>
<p><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/labels/install.png" alt="Install"></p>
<table style="width:100%;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px"><tbody><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">1</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Download the file from this page and open it with Minecraft.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">2</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Create or edit a world and turn on the new pack(s) in its settings.</td></tr><tr><td style="width:44px;text-align:center"><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;font-weight:bold;font-size:15px;text-align:center;border-radius:10px;width:30px;aspect-ratio:1/1;line-height:30px">3</div></td><td style="text-align:left;color:#f3e8ff">Play. No Experimental toggles needed.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div style="background-color:#1c1033;border:1px solid #581c87;border-radius:16px;text-align:center"><br><div style="background-color:#7e22ce;color:#ffffff;border-radius:20px;font-size:15px;line-height:2;display:inline-block">&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong>Found a Bug or Have a Suggestion?</strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;</div><p style="font-size:14px;color:#f3e8ff">Please leave a comment on this project page! All bug reports, gameplay feedback, and pack suggestions are welcome and appreciated.</p></div>
```

## AI Image Prompts

### Reference Images

- **Logo reference**: attach your own logo. The generator uses it only for brand colors and mood. The result must be a new design, not a copy.
- **Snapshot references**: attach 1 to 4 of your in-game snapshots of this pack. The generator copies their look, not their screen layout.

### Logo Prompt

```text
Use the attached logo only as a brand reference for its color palette and overall mood. Do not copy it. Create a new, original logo with a different composition and a different main symbol. Do not just recolor, resize, crop, mirror or tweak the attached logo.
Create a clean, square (1:1) logo image for the Minecraft Bedrock add-on "Vanilla Quality of Life". Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.
Base the main symbol on the pack idea above. Match the blocky look of the attached in-game snapshots. Center the logo, use a simple background, and keep it readable at small sizes.
Text: "Vanilla Quality of Life" and, in small letters, "ALNStudios". ALNStudios is the brand name, so always keep it and never remove or change it. Spell both exactly.
Do not add Minecraft, Mojang or Microsoft logos, watermarks, or characters from other games.
```

### Banner Prompt

```text
Create a wide (16:9) banner image for the Minecraft Bedrock add-on "Vanilla Quality of Life". Auto-switching tools, 128-log tree felling, and 64-ore vein mining with Tree Capitator and Domino Ore Miner for Minecraft Bedrock.
Use the attached in-game snapshots as scene and style references: copy their blocky look, lighting, colors and the kind of scenery they show. Make a new composition inspired by them. Do not copy the on-screen HUD, chat or hotbar.
Use the attached logo only as a brand reference for its colors and mood. Do not copy it. Put a new, original logo design in the banner, large enough to read, on the left or center.
Hint at these pack features visually: Tool and Weapon Auto-Switch, Tree Capitator, Tree Replanting, Leaf Clearing, Faster Crop Growth.
Text: "Vanilla Quality of Life" and, in small letters, "by ALNStudios". ALNStudios is the brand name, so always keep it and never remove or change it. Spell both exactly.
Leave clear space around the logo. No Minecraft, Mojang or Microsoft logos, no watermarks.
```
